FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\mentorship\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { CalendarDays, Clock } from "lucide-react";
import { Button, Card, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

type MentorSlot = {
  id: string;
  instructorName: string;
  expertise: string;
  rate: string;
  slots: string[];
};

const MENTORS: MentorSlot[] = [
  {
    id: "m1",
    instructorName: "Ayesha Khan",
    expertise: "AI Automation & Workflow Tools",
    rate: "$40 / 30 min",
    slots: ["Mon 10:00 AM", "Wed 2:00 PM", "Fri 11:00 AM"]
  },
  {
    id: "m2",
    instructorName: "Hamza Malik",
    expertise: "AI Integration for Developers",
    rate: "$55 / 45 min",
    slots: ["Tue 4:00 PM", "Thu 9:00 AM"]
  }
];

export default function MentorshipBookingPage() {
  const [selectedMentor, setSelectedMentor] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [selectedSlot, setSelectedSlot] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [booked, setBooked] = useState(false);

  const mentor = useMemo(() => MENTORS.find((m) => m.id === selectedMentor), [selectedMentor]);

  function handleBook() {
    if (!mentor || !selectedSlot) return;
    // NOTE: demo-only booking, no payment/calendar integration wired up yet.
    setBooked(true);
  }

  if (booked && mentor) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-lg py-16 text-center">
        <EmptyState
          title="Session booked"
          description={`Your session with ${mentor.instructorName} on ${selectedSlot} is confirmed. Check My Mentorship Sessions for the call link.`}
          action={
            <Link href="/mentorship/sessions">
              <Button>View my sessions</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-1 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Book mentorship</h1>
      <p className="mb-8 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">1-on-1 sessions with verified instructors.</p>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        {MENTORS.map((m) => (
          <Card
            key={m.id}
            className={`cursor-pointer p-5 transition-colors ${
              selectedMentor === m.id ? "border-gold-dark/60" : ""
            }`}
            onClick={() => {
              setSelectedMentor(m.id);
              setSelectedSlot(null);
            }}
          >
            <h3 className="text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{m.instructorName}</h3>
            <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{m.expertise}</p>
            <p className="mt-2 font-mono text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{m.rate}</p>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>

      {mentor && (
        <div className="mt-8 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
          <h2 className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
            <CalendarDays size={16} />
            Available slots with {mentor.instructorName}
          </h2>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {mentor.slots.map((slot) => (
              <button
                key={slot}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setSelectedSlot(slot)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-control border px-3 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium transition-colors ${
                  selectedSlot === slot
                    ? "border-gold-dark bg-gold-tint text-ink"
                    : "border-line text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
                }`}
              >
                <Clock size={13} />
                {slot}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          <Button className="mt-6" disabled={!selectedSlot} onClick={handleBook}>
            Confirm booking
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\my-courses\page.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { useEnrollmentStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState, Select } from "@saachi/ui";

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "All", value: "all" },
  { label: "In progress", value: "in_progress" },
  { label: "Completed", value: "completed" },
  { label: "Not started", value: "not_started" }
];

export default function MyCoursesPage() {
  const enrollments = useEnrollmentStore((s) => s.enrollments);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("all");

  const filtered = enrollments.filter((e) => {
    if (status === "in_progress") return e.progressPercent > 0 && e.progressPercent < 100;
    if (status === "completed") return e.progressPercent === 100;
    if (status === "not_started") return e.progressPercent === 0;
    return true;
  });

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">My Courses</h1>
        <Select options={STATUS_OPTIONS} value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} className="w-48" />
      </div>

      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="No courses here yet"
          description="Courses you enroll in will show up here."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {filtered.map((e) => (
            <Link
              key={e.courseId}
              href={`/learn/${e.courseId}/lesson`}
              className="flex flex-col overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card transition-colors hover:border-gold-dark/40"
            >
              {e.thumbnailUrl && (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img src={e.thumbnailUrl} alt={e.title} className="aspect-video w-full object-cover" />
              )}
              <div className="p-4">
                <h3 className="line-clamp-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">{e.title}</h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{e.instructorName}</p>
                <div className="mt-3 h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-paper-dim">
                  <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-gold" style={{ width: `${e.progressPercent}%` }} />
                </div>
                <p className="mt-1.5 text-[12px] text-ink-soft">{e.progressPercent}% complete</p>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Bell } from "lucide-react";
import { useNotificationStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState, cn } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function NotificationsPage() {
  const items = useNotificationStore((s) => s.items);
  const markRead = useNotificationStore((s) => s.markRead);
  const markAllRead = useNotificationStore((s) => s.markAllRead);

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Notifications</h1>
        <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={markAllRead}>
          Mark all as read
        </Button>
      </div>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No notifications" description="You're all caught up." />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          {items.map((n) => (
            <button
              key={n.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => markRead(n.id)}
              className={cn(
                "flex items-start gap-3 rounded-card border border-line p-4 text-left transition-colors",
                n.read ? "bg-card" : "bg-gold-tint/40"
              )}
            >
              <div className="mt-0.5 flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-paper-dim text-ink-soft">
                <Bell size={14} />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">{n.title}</p>
                <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{n.body}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-[12px] text-ink-soft/60">
                  {new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { dateStyle: "medium", timeStyle: "short" }).format(
                    new Date(n.createdAt)
                  )}
                </p>
              </div>
              {!n.read && <span className="mt-1.5 h-2 w-2 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-gold-dark" />}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { Suspense } from "react";
import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
import { CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useOrderStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

function OrderSuccessBanner() {
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const successId = searchParams.get("success");

  if (!successId) return null;

  return (
    <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-card border border-teal/30 bg-teal-light px-4 py-3">
      <CheckCircle2 size={18} className="text-teal" />
      <p className="text-[14px] text-ink">
        Payment successful. Order <span className="font-semibold">{successId}</span> is confirmed - your
        courses are now available in My Courses.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function OrdersTable() {
  const orders = useOrderStore((s) => s.orders);

  if (orders.length === 0) {
    return <EmptyState title="No orders yet" description="Your purchase history will show up here." />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
      <table className="w-full text-left text-[13px]">
        <thead className="border-b border-line bg-paper-dim">
          <tr>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Order</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Items</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Total</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Status</th>
            <th className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-ink-soft">Date</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {orders.map((order) => (
            <tr key={order.id} className="border-b border-line last:border-0">
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-medium text-ink">{order.id}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">{order.items.map((i) => i.title).join(", ")}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 font-mono text-ink">
                {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: order.currency }).format(order.total)}
              </td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 capitalize text-ink-soft">{order.status}</td>
              <td className="px-4 py-3.5 text-ink-soft">
                {new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", { dateStyle: "medium" }).format(new Date(order.createdAt))}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function OrdersPage() {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Order History</h1>

      <Suspense fallback={null}>
        <OrderSuccessBanner />
      </Suspense>

      <OrdersTable />
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { Download, ShieldAlert, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function SettingsPrivacyPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const [cookiePrefs, setCookiePrefs] = useState({ analytics: true, marketing: false });
  const [exportRequested, setExportRequested] = useState(false);

    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to view and edit your privacy settings." />;
  }

          <label className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Analytics cookies</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={cookiePrefs.analytics}
              onChange={(e) => setCookiePrefs((p) => ({ ...p, analytics: e.target.checked }))}
              aria-label="Toggle analytics cookies"
              className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
            />
          </label>
          <label className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Marketing cookies</span>
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={cookiePrefs.marketing}
              onChange={(e) => setCookiePrefs((p) => ({ ...p, marketing: e.target.checked }))}
              aria-label="Toggle marketing cookies"
              className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
            />
          </label>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
          <Download size={16} />
          Data export
        </h2>
        <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
          Request a copy of the personal data associated with your account.
        </p>
        <Button variant="secondary" disabled={exportRequested} onClick={() => setExportRequested(true)}>
          {exportRequested ? "Export requested" : "Request data export"}
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-error/30 bg-error-tint p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">

          Delete account

          Request account deletion
        </Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { ShieldCheck, Smartphone } from "lucide-react";
import { useSession } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Button, EmptyState, Input } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function SettingsSecurityPage() {
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const [twoFactorEnabled, setTwoFactorEnabled] = useState(false);
  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState("");
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState("");

  if (!session) {
    return <EmptyState title="Not logged in" description="Log in to view and edit your security settings." />;
  }

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend auth mutation wired up yet.
    setCurrentPassword("");
    setNewPassword("");
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Security</h1>

      <div className="max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
          <ShieldCheck size={16} />
          Change password
        </h2>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex flex-col gap-4">
          <Input
            label="Current password"
            type="password"
            value={currentPassword}
            onChange={(e) => setCurrentPassword(e.target.value)}
          />
          <Input
            label="New password"
            type="password"
            value={newPassword}
            onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
          />
          <Button type="submit" className="self-start">
            Update password
          </Button>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Smartphone size={16} className="text-ink-soft" />
            <div>
              <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-ink">Two-factor authentication</p>
              <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">Add an extra layer of security to your account.</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={twoFactorEnabled}
            onChange={(e) => setTwoFactorEnabled(e.target.checked)}
            aria-label="Toggle two-factor authentication"
            className="h-4 w-4 accent-gold-dark"
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 max-w-lg rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">Active sessions</h2>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">This device - {session.email}</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(learner)\wishlist\page.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { X } from "lucide-react";
import { useCartStore, useWishlistStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { Button, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function WishlistPage() {
  const items = useWishlistStore((s) => s.items);
  const removeFromWishlist = useWishlistStore((s) => s.remove);
  const addToCart = useCartStore((s) => s.addItem);

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Wishlist</h1>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title="Your wishlist is empty"
          description="Save courses you're interested in to find them here later."
          action={
            <Link href="/courses">
              <Button>Browse courses</Button>
            </Link>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.courseId}
              className="flex items-center gap-4 rounded-card border border-line bg-card p-4"
            >
              {item.thumbnailUrl && (
                // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
                <img
                  src={item.thumbnailUrl}
                  alt={item.title}
                  className="h-16 w-28 shrink-0 rounded-control object-cover"
                />
              )}
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <Link href={`/courses/${item.slug}`} className="truncate text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink hover:underline">
                  {item.title}
                </Link>
                <p className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{item.instructorName}</p>
              </div>
              <span className="font-mono text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
                {new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", { style: "currency", currency: item.currency }).format(item.price)}
              </span>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                onClick={() =>
                  addToCart({
                    courseId: item.courseId,
                    slug: item.slug,
                    title: item.title,
                    thumbnailUrl: item.thumbnailUrl,
                    instructorName: item.instructorName,
                    price: item.price,
                    currency: item.currency
                  })
                }
              >
                Add to cart
              </Button>
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-label="Remove from wishlist"
                onClick={() => removeFromWishlist(item.courseId)}
                className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
              >
                <X size={16} />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { BookOpen } from "lucide-react";
import { useBlogList } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Input, Loading, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function BlogListPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const { data, isLoading } = useBlogList({ search: search || undefined });

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-14 md:px-8">
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-2">
        <BookOpen size={22} className="text-accent-violet" />
        <h1 className="font-display text-[28px] font-bold text-ink">Blog</h1>
      </div>
      <Input
        placeholder="Search blog posts..."
        value={search}
        onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
        className="mb-8 max-w-sm"
      />

      {isLoading ? (
        <Loading label="Loading posts..." />
      ) : !data || data.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No posts found" description="Try a different search term." />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {data.map((post) => (
            <Link
              key={post.id}
              href={`/blog/${post.slug}`}
              className="group overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card transition-all hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg"
            >
              <div className="aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden">
                {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
                <img src={post.imageUrl} alt={post.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105" />
              </div>
              <div className="p-5">
                <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-gold-tint px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-gold-dark">{post.tag}</span>
                  <span className="text-[11.5px] text-ink-soft">{post.readTime}</span>
                </div>
                <h3 className="line-clamp-2 text-[15px] font-semibold leading-snug text-ink">{post.title}</h3>
                <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{post.excerpt}</p>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\courses\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useCourseList, type CourseListFilters } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { CourseCard, EmptyState, FilterSidebar, Input, Select, Loading, type FilterSidebarValue } from "@saachi/ui";
import { CATEGORIES } from "../../../lib/categories";

const SORT_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Most popular", value: "popular" },
  { label: "Newest", value: "newest" },
  { label: "Price: low to high", value: "price_asc" },
  { label: "Price: high to low", value: "price_desc" },
  { label: "Highest rated", value: "rating" }
];

const LEVEL_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "Beginner", value: "beginner" },
  { label: "Intermediate", value: "intermediate" },
  { label: "Advanced", value: "advanced" }
];

const LANGUAGE_OPTIONS = [
  { label: "English", value: "en" },
  { label: "Urdu", value: "ur" }
];

export default function CoursesPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState("popular");
  const [filters, setFilters] = useState<FilterSidebarValue>({ categories: [], level: undefined, language: undefined });

  const queryFilters: CourseListFilters = useMemo(
    () => ({
      search: search || undefined,
      category: filters.categories[0],
      level: filters.level,
      language: filters.language,
      sort
    }),
    [search, filters, sort]
  );

  const { data, isLoading } = useCourseList(queryFilters);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 md:px-8">
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[28px] font-bold text-ink">Course Marketplace</h1>

      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
        <Input
          placeholder="Search courses or instructors..."
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="md:max-w-sm"
        />
        <Select
          options={SORT_OPTIONS}
          value={sort}
          onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value)}
          className="md:w-56"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-8 md:grid-cols-[260px_1fr]">
        <FilterSidebar
          categoryOptions={CATEGORIES.map((c) => ({ label: c.name, value: c.id }))}
          levelOptions={LEVEL_OPTIONS}
          languageOptions={LANGUAGE_OPTIONS}
          value={filters}
          onChange={setFilters}
        />

        <div>
          {isLoading ? (
            <Loading label="Loading courses..." />
          ) : !data || data.items.length === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState title="No courses found" description="Try adjusting your filters or search term." />
          ) : (
            <>
              <p className="mb-4 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{data.total} courses found</p>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                {data.items.map((course) => (
                  <CourseCard
                    key={course.id}
                    slug={course.slug}
                    title={course.title}
                    thumbnailUrl={course.thumbnailUrl}
                    instructorName={course.instructor.fullName}
                    price={course.price}
                    currency={course.currency}
                    rating={course.rating}
                    ratingCount={course.ratingCount}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

    <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 py-16 md:px-0">
      <h1 className="mb-6 font-display text-[30px] font-bold text-ink">Instructor Agreement</h1>
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">
        <p>
          This Instructor Agreement governs the relationship between Saachi AI and instructors
          who create, publish, and sell courses on the Platform. By submitting a course for

        </p>

        <h2 className="mt-4 font-display text-[18px] font-semibold text-ink">1. Content ownership</h2>

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Newspaper } from "lucide-react";
import { useNewsList } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Input, Loading, EmptyState } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function NewsListPage() {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const { data, isLoading } = useNewsList({ search: search || undefined });

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-14 md:px-8">
      <div className="mb-8 flex items-center gap-2">
        <Newspaper size={22} className="text-accent-violet" />
        <h1 className="font-display text-[28px] font-bold text-ink">News</h1>
      </div>
      <Input
        placeholder="Search news..."
        value={search}
        onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
        className="mb-8 max-w-sm"
      />

      {isLoading ? (
        <Loading label="Loading news..." />
      ) : !data || data.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="No articles found" description="Try a different search term." />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {data.map((article) => (
            <Link
              key={article.id}
              href={`/news/${article.slug}`}
              className="group overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card transition-all hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg"
            >
              <div className="aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden">
                {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
                <img src={article.imageUrl} alt={article.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105" />
              </div>
              <div className="p-5">
                <div className="mb-2 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-gold-tint px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-gold-dark">{article.category}</span>
                  <span className="text-[11.5px] text-ink-soft">{article.readTime}</span>
                </div>
                <h3 className="line-clamp-2 text-[15px] font-semibold leading-snug text-ink">{article.title}</h3>
                <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{article.excerpt}</p>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\search\page.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { Suspense, useState } from "react";
import { useSearchParams, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import Link from "next/link";
import * as Tabs from "@radix-ui/react-tabs";
import {
  Search as SearchIcon,
  GraduationCap,
  Award,
  Newspaper,
  BookOpen,
  Users,
  Star
} from "lucide-react";
import {
  useCourseList,
  useNewsList,
  useBlogList,
  useCommunityList,
  useCertificateList
} from "@saachi/api-client";
import { CourseCard, EmptyState, Loading } from "@saachi/ui";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const TABS = [
  { value: "all",          label: "All",          icon: SearchIcon    },
  { value: "courses",      label: "Courses",      icon: GraduationCap },
  { value: "news",         label: "News",         icon: Newspaper     },
  { value: "blog",         label: "Blog",         icon: BookOpen      },
  { value: "community",    label: "Community",    icon: Users         },
  { value: "certificates", label: "Certificates", icon: Award         }
] as const;

type TabValue = (typeof TABS)[number]["value"];

/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ small reusable card Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */
function ContentCard({
  href,
  title,
  sub,
  tag,
  img
}: {
  href: string;
  title: string;
  sub?: string;
  tag?: string;
  img?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      className="group flex gap-4 rounded-xl border border-line bg-card p-4 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-gold-dark/40 hover:shadow-md"
    >
      {img && (
        // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
        <img
          src={img}
          alt={title}
          className="h-16 w-24 shrink-0 rounded-lg object-cover"
        />
      )}
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        {tag && (
          <span className="mb-1.5 inline-block rounded-full bg-gold-tint px-2 py-0.5 text-[10.5px] font-semibold text-gold-dark">
            {tag}
          </span>
        )}
        <h3 className="line-clamp-2 text-[14px] font-semibold leading-snug text-ink group-hover:text-accent-violet transition-colors">
          {title}
        </h3>
        {sub && <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">{sub}</p>}
      </div>
    </Link>
  );
}

/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ section heading Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */
function SectionHeading({ icon: Icon, label }: { icon: typeof SearchIcon; label: string }) {
  return (
    <h2 className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-[15px] font-semibold text-ink">
      <Icon size={16} className="text-accent-violet" />
      {label}
    </h2>
  );
}

/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ main content Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */
function SearchResults() {
  const router       = useRouter();
  const searchParams = useSearchParams();
  const q0           = searchParams.get("q") ?? "";
  const tab0         = (searchParams.get("type") ?? "all") as TabValue;

  const [inputVal, setInputVal] = useState(q0);
  const [tab, setTab]           = useState<TabValue>(tab0);

  const courseQ = useCourseList({ search: q0 || undefined });
  const newsQ   = useNewsList({ search: q0 || undefined });
  const blogQ   = useBlogList({ search: q0 || undefined });
  const commQ   = useCommunityList({ search: q0 || undefined });
  const certQ   = useCertificateList({ search: q0 || undefined });

  const loading = courseQ.isLoading || newsQ.isLoading || blogQ.isLoading || commQ.isLoading || certQ.isLoading;

  const courses = courseQ.data?.items ?? [];
  const news    = newsQ.data ?? [];
  const blog    = blogQ.data ?? [];
  const comm    = commQ.data ?? [];
  const certs   = certQ.data ?? [];

  const total   = courses.length + news.length + blog.length + comm.length + certs.length;

  function handleSearch(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const p = new URLSearchParams();
    if (inputVal.trim()) p.set("q", inputVal.trim());
    if (tab !== "all") p.set("type", tab);
    router.push(`/search?${p.toString()}`);
  }

  function changeTab(v: string) {
    setTab(v as TabValue);
    const p = new URLSearchParams();
    if (q0) p.set("q", q0);
    if (v !== "all") p.set("type", v);
    router.push(`/search?${p.toString()}`, { scroll: false });
  }

  const show = (key: TabValue) => tab === "all" || tab === key;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-10 md:px-8">
      {/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ search bar Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */}
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="mb-4 font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-ink">Search</h1>
        <form onSubmit={handleSearch} className="flex max-w-lg gap-2">
          <div className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line-strong bg-card px-4 py-2.5 shadow-sm focus-within:border-accent-violet transition-colors">
            <SearchIcon size={16} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft" />
            <input
              value={inputVal}
              onChange={(e) => setInputVal(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search courses, news, blog, community..."
              className="flex-1 bg-transparent text-[14px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:outline-none"
            />
          </div>
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="rounded-full px-5 py-2.5 text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:brightness-110"
            style={{ backgroundImage: "var(--color-accent-gradient)" }}
          >
            Search
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      {/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ tabs (Radix) Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */}
      <Tabs.Root value={tab} onValueChange={changeTab}>
        <Tabs.List className="mb-8 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 border-b border-line pb-0">
          {TABS.map(({ value, label, icon: Icon }) => (
            <Tabs.Trigger
              key={value}
              value={value}
              className={cn(
                "relative flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-t-lg px-3.5 py-2 text-[12.5px] font-semibold transition-colors",
                "text-ink-soft hover:text-ink",
                "data-[state=active]:text-accent-violet",
                // animated bottom border
                "after:absolute after:bottom-[-1px] after:left-0 after:right-0 after:h-[2px] after:rounded-t-full after:bg-accent-violet after:scale-x-0 after:transition-transform",
                "data-[state=active]:after:scale-x-100"
              )}
            >
              <Icon size={13} />
              {label}
            </Tabs.Trigger>
          ))}
        </Tabs.List>

        {/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ results Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */}
        <Tabs.Content value={tab} forceMount className="outline-none">
          {!q0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              title="Search Saachi AI"
              description="Type a course, topic, article, or instructor name above."
            />
          ) : loading ? (
            <Loading label="Searching..." />
          ) : total === 0 ? (
            <EmptyState
              title={`No results for "${q0}"`}
              description="Try a different search term."
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-12">
              {/* Courses */}
              {show("courses") && courses.length > 0 && (
                <section>
                  <SectionHeading icon={GraduationCap} label="Courses" />
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                    {courses.map((c) => (
                      <CourseCard
                        key={c.id}
                        slug={c.slug}
                        title={c.title}
                        thumbnailUrl={c.thumbnailUrl}
                        instructorName={c.instructor.fullName}
                        price={c.price}
                        currency={c.currency}
                        rating={c.rating}
                        ratingCount={c.ratingCount}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </section>
              )}

              {/* News */}
              {show("news") && news.length > 0 && (
                <section>
                  <SectionHeading icon={Newspaper} label="News" />
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                    {news.map((a) => (
                      <ContentCard
                        key={a.id}
                        href={`/news/${a.slug}`}
                        title={a.title}
                        sub={a.excerpt}
                        tag={a.category}
                        img={a.imageUrl}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </section>
              )}

              {/* Blog */}
              {show("blog") && blog.length > 0 && (
                <section>
                  <SectionHeading icon={BookOpen} label="Blog" />
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                    {blog.map((b) => (
                      <ContentCard
                        key={b.id}
                        href={`/blog/${b.slug}`}
                        title={b.title}
                        sub={b.excerpt}
                        tag={b.tag}
                        img={b.imageUrl}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </section>
              )}

              {/* Community */}
              {show("community") && comm.length > 0 && (
                <section>
                  <SectionHeading icon={Users} label="Community" />
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                    {comm.map((p) => (
                      <ContentCard
                        key={p.id}
                        href={`/community/${p.id}`}
                        title={p.title}
                        sub={p.body}
                        tag={p.category}
                      />
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </section>
              )}

              {/* Certificates */}
              {show("certificates") && certs.length > 0 && (
                <section>
                  <SectionHeading icon={Award} label="Certificates" />
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                    {certs.map((c) => (
                      <Link
                        key={c.id}
                        href={`/certificates/${c.id}`}
                        className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-line bg-card p-4 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-gold-dark/40 hover:shadow-md"
                      >
                        <Award size={20} className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-gold-dark" />
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <p className="font-semibold text-[14px] text-ink">{c.courseTitle}</p>
                          <p className="mt-0.5 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">
                            {c.learnerName} &middot; {c.verificationId}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </Link>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </section>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </Tabs.Content>
      </Tabs.Root>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function SearchPage() {
  return (
    <Suspense fallback={<Loading label="Loading..." />}>
      <SearchResults />
    </Suspense>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Sparkle } from "lucide-react";
import { ACTIVITY_FEED } from "./landing-fake-data";

export function ActivityTicker() {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => {
      setIndex((i) => (i + 1) % ACTIVITY_FEED.length);
    }, 3200);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, []);

  const item = ACTIVITY_FEED[index];

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex w-fit max-w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-line bg-card px-4 py-2 shadow-sm">
      <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-teal-light text-teal">
        <Sparkle size={11} />
      </span>
      <span key={item.id} className="truncate text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft duration-300 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-1">
        {item.text}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react";

const FAQS = [
  {
    q: "Do I need any prior AI experience?",
    a: "No. Courses are organized by level -- Beginner, Intermediate, and Advanced -- so you can start exactly where you are."
  },
  {
    q: "Can I really earn money teaching on Saachi AI?",
    a: "Yes. Instructors set their own pricing and keep the majority of each sale after the platform commission, disclosed upfront at course submission."
  },
  {
    q: "Are certificates recognized?",
    a: "Every completed course issues a shareable completion certificate you can add to LinkedIn or your portfolio."
  },
  {
    q: "What if a course isn't what I expected?",
    a: "We offer refunds within 7 days of purchase, as long as you haven't completed more than 30% of the content."
  },
  {
    q: "Can I book 1-on-1 time with an instructor?",
    a: "Many instructors offer paid mentorship sessions -- book directly from their profile once you're enrolled."
  }
];

export function FaqAccordion() {
  const [openIndex, setOpenIndex] = useState<number | null>(0);

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col divide-y divide-line rounded-card border border-line bg-card">
      {FAQS.map((item, i) => {
        const open = openIndex === i;
        return (
          <div key={item.q}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setOpenIndex(open ? null : i)}
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-4 px-5 py-4 text-left"
            >
              <span className="text-[14.5px] font-semibold text-ink">{item.q}</span>
              <ChevronDown
                size={17}
                className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft transition-transform duration-200"
                style={{ transform: open ? "rotate(180deg)" : "rotate(0deg)" }}
              />
            </button>
            <div
              className="overflow-hidden px-5 text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft transition-all duration-200 ease-out"
              style={{
                maxHeight: open ? "200px" : "0px",
                paddingBottom: open ? "16px" : "0px",
                opacity: open ? 1 : 0
              }}
            >
              {item.a}
            </div>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\_components\LandingSearch.tsx

"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { Search, TrendingUp, Star } from "lucide-react";

type FakeResult = {
  title: string;
  category: string;
  instructor: string;
  rating: number;
  students: number;
};

// NOTE: demo-only dummy dataset for the landing-page search preview.
const FAKE_COURSES: FakeResult[] = [
  { title: "Automate Everything with n8n & AI", category: "Automation", instructor: "Ayesha Khan", rating: 4.8, students: 2140 },
  { title: "Prompt Engineering for Client Work", category: "Fundamentals", instructor: "Hamza Malik", rating: 4.6, students: 980 },
  { title: "AI Chatbots for WhatsApp Support", category: "Automation", instructor: "Ayesha Khan", rating: 4.7, students: 1560 },
  { title: "Cold Outreach with Claude", category: "Marketing", instructor: "Sara Iqbal", rating: 4.5, students: 740 },
  { title: "Build AI Agents with LangChain", category: "Development", instructor: "Omar Sheikh", rating: 4.9, students: 3200 },
  { title: "AI Design Systems in Figma", category: "Design", instructor: "Noor Fatima", rating: 4.7, students: 1120 },
  { title: "Freelancing with AI Tools", category: "Career", instructor: "Bilal S.", rating: 4.4, students: 610 },
  { title: "Voice AI & Call Automation", category: "Automation", instructor: "Hina Malik", rating: 4.6, students: 890 }
];

export function LandingSearch() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false);

  const results = useMemo(() => {
    if (!query.trim()) return [];
    const q = query.toLowerCase();
    return FAKE_COURSES.filter(
      (c) =>
        c.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        c.category.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        c.instructor.toLowerCase().includes(q)
    ).slice(0, 5);
  }, [query]);

  const showDropdown = focused && query.trim().length > 0;

  return (
    <div className="relative mx-auto w-full max-w-xl">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-full border border-line bg-card px-5 py-3.5 shadow-sm transition-shadow focus-within:shadow-md">
        <Search size={18} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft" />
        <input
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          onFocus={() => setFocused(true)}
          onBlur={() => setTimeout(() => setFocused(false), 150)}
          placeholder="Search courses, skills, or instructors..."
          className="w-full bg-transparent text-[14px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-soft focus:outline-none"
        />
        {query && (
          <span className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-soft">
            {results.length} results
          </span>
        )}
      </div>

      {showDropdown && (
        <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-[calc(100%+8px)] z-30 overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card shadow-lg">
          {results.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="px-5 py-6 text-center text-[13px] text-ink-soft">
              No matches for &quot;{query}&quot; -- try &quot;automation&quot; or &quot;design&quot;.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <ul className="flex flex-col divide-y divide-line">
              {results.map((r) => (
                <li key={r.title}>
                  <Link
                    href="/courses"
                    className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-5 py-3 transition-colors hover:bg-paper-dim"
                  >
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <p className="truncate text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-ink">{r.title}</p>
                      <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-soft">
                        {r.category} - {r.instructor}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 text-[12px] font-medium text-ink-soft">
                      <Star size={12} className="fill-current text-accent-pink" />
                      {r.rating}
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}
          <Link
            href="/courses"
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-t border-line bg-paper-dim px-5 py-3 text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-accent-violet hover:underline"
          >
            <TrendingUp size={13} />
            See all courses matching &quot;{query}&quot;
          </Link>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Mail, CheckCircle2 } from "lucide-react";

export function NewsletterCard() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false);

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    // NOTE: demo-only, no backend newsletter-signup endpoint wired up yet.
    setSubmitted(true);
  }

  return (
    <div
      className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-card p-8 text-center md:p-12"
      style={{ backgroundImage: "var(--color-accent-gradient)" }}
    >
      <Mail size={26} className="mx-auto mb-3 text-white/90" />
      <h2 className="font-display text-[22px] font-bold text-white md:text-[26px]">
        Get new courses in your inbox
      </h2>
      <p className="mx-auto mt-2 max-w-md text-[13.5px] text-white/85">
        One email a week. New releases, instructor spotlights, and the occasional discount code.
      </p>

      {submitted ? (
        <div className="mx-auto mt-6 flex w-fit items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-white/15 px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-white">
          <CheckCircle2 size={16} />
          You&apos;re on the list -- check your inbox.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mx-auto mt-6 flex max-w-md flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
          <input
            type="email"
            required
            value={email}
            onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="you@example.com"
            className="w-full rounded-full border-0 px-4 py-2.5 text-[13.5px] text-ink outline-none placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-white/60"
          />
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="shrink-0 rounded-full bg-white px-5 py-2.5 text-[13.5px] font-semibold text-accent-violet transition-transform hover:scale-105"
          >
            Subscribe
          </button>
        </form>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\_components\Reveal.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";

export function Reveal({
  children,
  delay = 0,
  className = ""
}: {
  children: ReactNode;
  delay?: number;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        entries.forEach((entry) => {
          if (entry.isIntersecting) {
            setVisible(true);
            observer.disconnect();
          }
        });
      },
      { threshold: 0.15 }
    );
    observer.observe(el);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      className={className}
      style={{
        opacity: visible ? 1 : 0,
        transform: visible ? "translateY(0)" : "translateY(24px)",
        transition: `opacity 600ms ease-out ${delay}ms, transform 600ms ease-out ${delay}ms`
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

  },

    cta: "Talk to sales",
    href: "/contact",
    colors: ["#009fb3", "#7c3aed"] as [string, string]
  }
];

export function SpotlightPanels() {
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-3">
      {PANELS.map((p) => (
        <BorderBeamPanel key={p.title} beams={2} thickness={2} radius={18} colors={p.colors} className="!bg-card !border-line">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
            <span className="flex w-fit items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full border border-line px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.14em] text-ink-soft">
              <p.icon size={12} />
              {p.tag}
            </span>
            <h3 className="font-display text-[20px] font-semibold text-ink">{p.title}</h3>
            <p className="text-[13.5px] leading-relaxed text-ink-soft">{p.desc}</p>
            <Link
              href={p.href}
              className="mt-1 w-fit rounded-lg bg-ink px-4 py-2 text-[13px] font-medium text-paper transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
            >
              {p.cta}
            </Link>
          </div>
        </BorderBeamPanel>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\(marketing)\_components\TrendingCarousel.tsx

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRef } from "react";
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Star, Flame } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@saachi/ui";
import { TRENDING_COURSES } from "./landing-fake-data";

const BADGE_STYLES: Record<string, string> = {
  Bestseller: "bg-gold text-ink",
  New: "bg-teal text-white",
  Hot: "bg-accent-pink text-white",
  "Staff pick": "bg-accent-violet text-white"
};

export function TrendingCarousel() {
  const trackRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  function scrollBy(amount: number) {
    trackRef.current?.scrollBy({ left: amount, behavior: "smooth" });
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Flame size={18} className="text-accent-pink" />
          <h2 className="font-display text-[22px] font-semibold text-ink">Trending this week</h2>
        </div>
        <div className="hidden items-center gap-2 md:flex">
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Scroll left"
            onClick={() => scrollBy(-360)}
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-line bg-card text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
          >
            <ChevronLeft size={16} />
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Scroll right"
            onClick={() => scrollBy(360)}
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-line bg-card text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
          >
            <ChevronRight size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div
        ref={trackRef}
        className="flex snap-x snap-mandatory gap-4 overflow-x-auto pb-3 [-ms-overflow-style:none] [scrollbar-width:none] [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden"
      >
        {TRENDING_COURSES.map((c) => (
          <Link
            key={c.id}
            href="/courses"
            className="group relative w-[260px] shrink-0 snap-start overflow-hidden rounded-card border border-line bg-card transition-all hover:-translate-y-1 hover:shadow-lg"
          >
            <div className="relative aspect-video w-full overflow-hidden">
              {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
              <img src={c.image} alt={c.title} className="h-full w-full object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105" />
              <span className={cn("absolute left-3 top-3 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide", BADGE_STYLES[c.badge])}>
                {c.badge}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="p-4">
              <p className="text-[11px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-accent-violet">{c.category}</p>
              <h3 className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-[14.5px] font-semibold leading-snug text-ink">{c.title}</h3>
              <p className="mt-1 text-[12.5px] text-ink-soft">{c.instructor}</p>
              <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink-soft">
                  <Star size={12} className="fill-current text-accent-pink" />
                  {c.rating} ({c.students})
                </span>
                <span className="font-mono text-[14px] font-bold text-ink">{c.price}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

            <ShieldCheck size={28} className="mx-auto mb-4 text-white/90" />
            <h2 className="font-display text-[26px] font-bold text-white md:text-[34px]">
              Your next skill is one course away.
            </h2>
            <p className="mx-auto mt-3 max-w-lg text-[14.5px] text-white/85">
              Join 42,000+ learners already building real AI projects. 7-day refund window, no risk.
            </p>
            <div className="mt-7 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3">
              <Link href="/courses">
                <button className="rounded-full bg-white px-6 py-3 text-[14px] font-semibold text-accent-violet transition-transform hover:scale-105">
                  Start learning free
                </button>
              </Link>
              <Link href="/register">
                <button className="rounded-full border border-white/40 px-6 py-3 text-[14px] font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-white/10">
                  Become an instructor
                </button>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Reveal>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\_components\ConditionalChrome.tsx

"use client";

import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { SiteHeader } from "./SiteHeader";
import { SiteFooter } from "./SiteFooter";

// Routes that render their own header/shell (e.g. InstructorShell) and
// should NOT also get the public SiteHeader/SiteFooter.
const CHROME_EXCLUDED_PREFIXES = ["/instructor"];

export function ConditionalChrome({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const hideChrome = CHROME_EXCLUDED_PREFIXES.some((prefix) => pathname?.startsWith(prefix));

  if (hideChrome) {
    return <>{children}</>;
  }

  return (
    <>
      <SiteHeader />
      <main>{children}</main>
      <SiteFooter />
    </>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState } from "react";
import { ShoppingCart, Heart, Menu as MenuIcon, X, LogOut } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@saachi/ui";
import { useCartStore, useWishlistStore } from "@saachi/store";
import { AnimatedThemeToggler } from "@/components/ui/animated-theme-toggler";
import { useSession, useLogout } from "@saachi/api-client";
import { Menu, MenuItem, HoveredLink } from "@/components/ui/navbar-menu";
import { CATEGORIES } from "../../lib/categories";
import { TopSearchBar, useSearchBarHidden } from "./TopSearchBar";

const MOBILE_NAV_LINKS = [
  { href: "/courses",                   label: "All courses" },
  { href: "/categories/ai-automation",  label: "Automation" },
  { href: "/categories/ai-marketing",   label: "Marketing" },
  { href: "/pricing",                   label: "Pricing" },
  { href: "/about",                     label: "About" }
];

export function SiteHeader() {
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [active, setActive]         = useState<string | null>(null);

  const cartCount     = useCartStore((s) => s.items.length);
  const wishlistCount = useWishlistStore((s) => s.items.length);
  const { data: session } = useSession();
  const logout = useLogout();

  // single scroll listener Ã¢â‚¬â€ shared by TopSearchBar visibility
  const searchHidden = useSearchBarHidden();

  return (
    /*
     * The entire header is sticky. TopSearchBar lives INSIDE it so it
     * naturally hides behind the nav bar when collapsed Ã¢â‚¬â€ no z-index tricks.
     */
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 border-b border-line bg-card/90 backdrop-blur">
      {/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ top nav row Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */}
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 md:px-8">
        <Link href="/" className="font-display text-[20px] font-bold text-ink">
          Saachi<span className="text-gold-dark">AI</span>
        </Link>

        <div className="ml-3 hidden items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-line bg-paper-dim px-2.5 py-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-soft lg:flex">
          <span className="text-gold-dark">4.8</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">&#9733;</span>
          <span>Rated by learners</span>
        </div>

        {/* desktop nav */}
        <div className="hidden md:block">
          <Menu setActive={setActive}>
            <MenuItem setActive={setActive} active={active} item="Courses">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-10 gap-y-2 p-1 text-sm">
                {CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
                  <HoveredLink key={cat.id} href={`/categories/${cat.slug}`}>
                    {cat.name}
                  </HoveredLink>
                ))}
                <HoveredLink href="/courses" className="font-semibold text-gold-dark">
                  Browse all courses &rarr;
                </HoveredLink>
              </div>
            </MenuItem>

            <MenuItem setActive={setActive} active={active} item="For Instructors">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 text-sm">
                <HoveredLink href="/register">Become an instructor</HoveredLink>
                <HoveredLink href="/mentorship">Offer mentorship</HoveredLink>
                <HoveredLink href="/legal/instructor-agreement">Instructor agreement</HoveredLink>
              </div>
            </MenuItem>

            <MenuItem setActive={setActive} active={active} item="Company">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 text-sm">
                <HoveredLink href="/about">About us</HoveredLink>
                <HoveredLink href="/pricing">Pricing</HoveredLink>
                <HoveredLink href="/contact">Contact</HoveredLink>
              </div>
            </MenuItem>
          </Menu>
        </div>

        {/* right icons */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <AnimatedThemeToggler />

          <Link
            href="/wishlist"
            aria-label="Wishlist"
            className="relative flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
          >
            <Heart size={18} />
            {wishlistCount > 0 && (
              <span className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">
                {wishlistCount}
              </span>
            )}
          </Link>

          <Link
            href="/cart"
            aria-label="Cart"
            className="relative flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft hover:bg-paper-dim"
          >
            <ShoppingCart size={18} />
            {cartCount > 0 && (
              <span className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gold text-[10px] font-bold text-ink">
                {cartCount}
              </span>
            )}
          </Link>

          {session ? (
            <div className="hidden items-center gap-2 md:flex">
              <span className="text-[13px] text-ink-soft">{session.fullName}</span>
              <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => logout.mutate()} aria-label="Log out">
                <LogOut size={14} />
              </Button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="hidden items-center gap-2 md:flex">
              <Link href="/login">
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary">Log in</Button>
              </Link>
              <Link href="/register">
                <Button size="sm" variant="brand">Sign up</Button>
              </Link>
            </div>
          )}

          <button
            type="button"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-control text-ink-soft md:hidden"
            onClick={() => setMobileOpen((v) => !v)}
            aria-label="Toggle menu"
          >
            {mobileOpen ? <X size={20} /> : <MenuIcon size={20} />}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ search bar row Ã¢â‚¬â€ inside header, collapses on scroll Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */}
      <TopSearchBar hidden={searchHidden} />

      {/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ mobile nav Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */}
      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="border-t border-line bg-card px-4 py-4 md:hidden">
          <nav className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            {MOBILE_NAV_LINKS.map((link) => (
              <Link
                key={link.href}
                href={link.href}
                onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
                className="text-[15px] font-medium text-ink"
              >
                {link.label}
              </Link>
            ))}
            <div className="mt-2 flex gap-2">
              {session ? (
                <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => logout.mutate()} className="w-full">
                  Log out
                </Button>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Link href="/login" className="flex-1">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" className="w-full">Log in</Button>
                  </Link>
                  <Link href="/register" className="flex-1">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="brand" className="w-full">Sign up</Button>
                  </Link>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </nav>
        </div>
      )}
    </header>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: apps\web\app\_components\TopSearchBar.tsx
================================================================================
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { Search, ChevronDown, GraduationCap, Award, Newspaper, BookOpen, Users } from "lucide-react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  useCourseList,
  useNewsList,
  useBlogList,
  useCommunityList,
  useCertificateList
} from "@saachi/api-client";
import { CATEGORIES } from "../../lib/categories";
import { SearchCommand, type SearchGroup } from "@/components/ui/search-command";

/**
 * Returns true when the user is scrolling DOWN past 80 px.
 * Used by SiteHeader to collapse the search row.
 */
export function useSearchBarHidden() {
  const [hidden, setHidden] = useState(false);
  const lastY = useRef(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    lastY.current = window.scrollY;
    function onScroll() {
      const y = window.scrollY;
      setHidden(y > lastY.current && y > 80);
      lastY.current = y;
    }
    window.addEventListener("scroll", onScroll, { passive: true });
    return () => window.removeEventListener("scroll", onScroll);
  }, []);
  return hidden;
}

export function TopSearchBar({ hidden }: { hidden: boolean }) {
  const router = useRouter();

  const [query, setQuery]       = useState("");
  const [deptOpen, setDeptOpen] = useState(false);
  const [department, setDept]   = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const [open, setOpen]         = useState(false);

  const deptRef    = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const wrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const courseQ = useCourseList({ search: query || undefined, category: department, sort: "popular" });
  const newsQ   = useNewsList({ search: query || undefined });
  const blogQ   = useBlogList({ search: query || undefined });
  const commQ   = useCommunityList({ search: query || undefined });
  const certQ   = useCertificateList({ search: query || undefined });

  const groups: SearchGroup[] = useMemo(() => [
    {
      key: "courses",
      label: "Courses",
      icon: <GraduationCap size={12} />,
      items: (courseQ.data?.items ?? []).slice(0, 5).map((c) => ({
        id: c.id,
        title: c.title,
        href: `/courses/${c.slug}`,
        sub: c.instructor.fullName
      }))
    },
    {
      key: "news",
      label: "News",
      icon: <Newspaper size={12} />,
      items: (newsQ.data ?? []).slice(0, 3).map((n) => ({
        id: n.id,
        title: n.title,
        href: `/news/${n.slug}`,
        sub: n.category
      }))
    },
    {
      key: "blog",
      label: "Blog",
      icon: <BookOpen size={12} />,
      items: (blogQ.data ?? []).slice(0, 3).map((b) => ({
        id: b.id,
        title: b.title,
        href: `/blog/${b.slug}`,
        sub: b.tag
      }))
    },
    {
      key: "community",
      label: "Community",
      icon: <Users size={12} />,
      items: (commQ.data ?? []).slice(0, 3).map((p) => ({
        id: p.id,
        title: p.title,
        href: `/community/${p.id}`,
        sub: p.category
      }))
    },
    {
      key: "certificates",
      label: "Certificates",
      icon: <Award size={12} />,
      items: (certQ.data ?? []).slice(0, 3).map((c) => ({
        id: c.id,
        title: c.courseTitle,
        href: `/certificates/${c.id}`,
        sub: c.learnerName
      }))
    }
  ], [courseQ.data, newsQ.data, blogQ.data, commQ.data, certQ.data]);

  // close dept dropdown on outside click
  useEffect(() => {
    function handle(e: MouseEvent) {
      if (deptRef.current && !deptRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setDeptOpen(false);
    }
    document.addEventListener("mousedown", handle);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("mousedown", handle);
  }, []);

  function handleSubmit(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setOpen(false);
    const params = new URLSearchParams();
    if (query.trim()) params.set("q", query.trim());
    if (department)   params.set("category", department);
    router.push(`/search?${params.toString()}`);
  }

  const deptLabel = department
    ? CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.id === department)?.name ?? "Department"
    : "All depts";

  return (
    /*
     * overflow-hidden + max-h transition = smooth accordion collapse.
     * When hidden=true the bar slides up and disappears behind the nav bar
     * because the entire <header> is sticky Ã¢â‚¬â€ no z-index clipping needed.
     */
    <div
      className={cn(
        "overflow-hidden border-t border-black/10 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out",
        hidden ? "max-h-0 opacity-0" : "max-h-20 opacity-100"
      )}
      style={{ backgroundImage: "var(--color-accent-gradient)" }}
    >
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-7xl items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 md:px-8">
        {/* Search input + cmdk dropdown */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="relative flex-1 max-w-xl" ref={wrapperRef}>
          <div className="flex h-10 items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-white/25 bg-white pl-4 pr-2 shadow-sm">
            <Search size={15} className="shrink-0 text-ink-soft" />
            <input
              value={query}
              onChange={(e) => { setQuery(e.target.value); setOpen(true); }}
              onFocus={() => setOpen(true)}
              placeholder="Search courses, news, community..."
              className="w-full bg-transparent text-[13px] text-ink placeholder:text-ink-soft/60 focus:outline-none"
            />

            {/* Department picker */}
            <div ref={deptRef} className="relative shrink-0">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDeptOpen((v) => !v)}
                className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-paper-dim px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-ink-soft hover:text-ink transition-colors"
              >
                <span className="hidden max-w-[80px] truncate sm:inline">{deptLabel}</span>
                <ChevronDown size={11} className={cn("transition-transform duration-200", deptOpen && "rotate-180")} />
              </button>

              {deptOpen && (
                <div className="absolute right-0 top-[calc(100%+8px)] z-50 w-56 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line bg-card shadow-xl">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => { setDept(undefined); setDeptOpen(false); }}
                    className={cn("flex w-full items-center px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-[13px] hover:bg-paper-dim", !department ? "font-semibold text-accent-violet" : "text-ink")}
                  >
                    All departments
                  </button>
                  {CATEGORIES.map((cat) => (
                    <button
                      key={cat.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => { setDept(cat.id); setDeptOpen(false); }}
                      className={cn("flex w-full items-center px-4 py-2.5 text-left text-[13px] hover:bg-paper-dim", department === cat.id ? "font-semibold text-accent-violet" : "text-ink")}
                    >
                      {cat.name}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* cmdk dropdown Ã¢â‚¬â€ renders below the white pill */}
          {open && (
            <SearchCommand
              query={query}
              groups={groups}
              onClose={() => setOpen(false)}
            />
          )}
        </form>

        {/* Search submit button */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleSubmit as any}
          className="group/btn relative hidden h-10 shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-zinc-900 px-5 sm:flex"
        >
          <span className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 via-purple-500 to-pink-500 opacity-50 blur transition-opacity duration-500 group-hover/btn:opacity-90" />
          <span className="relative flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px] font-semibold text-white">
            <Search size={14} />
            Search
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default TopSearchBar;

FILE: apps\web\app\error.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { Button } from "@saachi/ui";

export default function GlobalError({
  error,
  reset
}: {
  error: Error & { digest?: string };
  reset: () => void;
}) {
  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line no-console
    console.error(error);
  }, [error]);

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-lg flex-col items-center px-4 py-24 text-center md:px-0">
      <span className="font-mono text-[64px] font-bold text-error">500</span>
      <h1 className="mt-2 font-display text-[24px] font-bold text-ink">Something went wrong</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-[14px] text-ink-soft">
        An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.
      </p>
      <div className="mt-8 flex gap-3">
        <Button onClick={() => reset()}>Try again</Button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps\web\app\providers.tsx

"use client";

import { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useThemeStore } from "@saachi/store";

function ThemeSync() {
  const theme = useThemeStore((s) => s.theme);

  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", theme);
  }, [theme]);

  return null;
}

export function Providers({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [client] = useState(() => new QueryClient({
    defaultOptions: { queries: { staleTime: 30_000, refetchOnWindowFocus: false } }
  }));

  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={client}>
      <ThemeSync />
      {children}
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}
